0001 — 3/4 sit-up

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0001 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A shortened sit-up that stops at about 45 degrees, keeping tension on the abs the whole time.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. Place your hands behind your head with your elbows pointing outwards.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie flat on your back with your knees bent and feet flat on the ground. 0001.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your hands behind your head with your elbows pointing outwards. 0001.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engaging your abs, slowly lift your upper body off the ground, curling forward until your torso is at a 45-degree angle. 0001.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your upper body back down to the starting position. 0001.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0001.json#/instructions/4

0002 — 45° side bend

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0002 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A standing side bend that works the obliques by leaning the torso sideways and coming back up.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms extended straight down by your sides.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your arms extended straight down by your sides. 0002.json#/instructions/0
  2. Keeping your back straight and your core engaged, slowly bend your torso to one side, lowering your hand towards your knee. 0002.json#/instructions/1
  3. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then slowly return to the starting position. 0002.json#/instructions/2
  4. Repeat on the other side. 0002.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 0002.json#/instructions/4

0003 — air bike

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0003 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: supine.

A bicycle-style crunch where opposite elbow and knee meet while the other leg straightens. Hits the abs and obliques.

Proposed staging

Lie flat on your back with your hands placed behind your head.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie flat on your back with your hands placed behind your head. 0003.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your legs off the ground and bend your knees at a 90-degree angle. 0003.json#/instructions/1
  3. Bring your right elbow towards your left knee while simultaneously straightening your right leg. 0003.json#/instructions/2
  4. Return to the starting position and repeat the movement on the opposite side, bringing your left elbow towards your right knee while straightening your left leg. 0003.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue alternating sides in a pedaling motion for the desired number of repetitions. 0003.json#/instructions/4

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0005 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A biceps curl done one arm at a time against a band stood on with your feet. It gives rising tension toward the top of the curl.

Proposed staging

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0006 after decompression) · Source record

Lying on your back, you crunch sideways to reach each hand toward its heel in turn. Works the obliques and abs.

0007 — alternate lateral pulldown

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0007 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A cable lat pulldown done one handle at a time, building the lats with a stretch and squeeze on each side.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on the cable machine with your back straight and feet flat on the ground. Grasp the handles with an overhand grip, slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

0010 — assisted hanging knee raise with throw down

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0010 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: hanging.

An assisted hanging knee raise that ends with the legs dropping back down under control. Trains the abs and hip flexors.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hang from a pull-up bar with your arms fully extended and your palms facing away from you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0010.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Hang from a pull-up bar with your arms fully extended and your palms facing away from you.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0010.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Keep the feet clear of the lower support surface and the legs quiet in a comfortable alignment; preserve source-defined knee bends or leg raises.

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Pull-up/inverted-row bar. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Hang from a pull-up bar with your arms fully extended and your palms facing away from you. 0010.json#/instructions/0
  2. Engage your core and lift your knees towards your chest, keeping your legs together. 0010.json#/instructions/1
  3. Once your knees are at chest level, explosively throw your legs down towards the ground, extending them fully. 0010.json#/instructions/2
  4. Allow your legs to swing back up and repeat the movement for the desired number of repetitions. 0010.json#/instructions/3

0102 — barbell squat (on knees)

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 0102 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: kneeling.

A squat performed from a kneeling position with the bar on the back. Trains the quads and glutes.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by kneeling on the ground with your knees hip-width apart and your toes pointing forward. Place a barbell across your shoulders, gripping it with an overhand grip and your hands slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0102.json#/instructions/0, 0102.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Place a barbell across your shoulders, gripping it with an overhand grip and your hands slightly wider than shoulder-width apart.

Start by kneeling on the ground with your knees hip-width apart and your toes pointing forward.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 0102.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Straight Olympic barbell. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 0102.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start by kneeling on the ground with your knees hip-width apart and your toes pointing forward. 0102.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place a barbell across your shoulders, gripping it with an overhand grip and your hands slightly wider than shoulder-width apart. 0102.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core and keep your chest lifted as you slowly lower your body down by bending your knees, keeping your back straight. 0102.json#/instructions/2
  4. Continue lowering until your thighs are parallel to the ground, or as low as you can comfortably go. 0102.json#/instructions/3
  5. Pause for a moment at the bottom, then push through your heels to return to the starting position. 0102.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 0102.json#/instructions/5
